# Implementation requirements matrix

Baseline is implementcode at 093ab0b. OR IDs refer to sections of the original attachment, and WS IDs track the sequential closure instructions. Related paths are starting points for the audit. PARTIALLY_IMPLEMENTED and NOT_TESTED are conservative where existing code and tests do not establish clause-level completion. Live execution is deferred under the latest instructions.

Original sections 2 through 4 describe roles, previously reported progress, and known limitations. Section 8's lifecycle diagram is tracked through the agent, gate, and recovery clauses that follow it.

The basic unified local flow now includes independent review, correction and post-approval validation. A disposable mocked-boundary check passed. Advanced requirement statuses below are tracked separately.
